In particular no hereditary or scalar\ncapacity theorem from those drafts is a premise.\n\nThe immutable input821f43aa871af2a2 was read and its SHA256 matched\n2b96fb4095e0fc931b8ad619667feacf6a636294ed25cea3e7fb6389bb6778a3.\nThe mechanism is a random walk of CERTIFICATE coefficients. It imposes no\nrandom, orthogonal, or Markov law on gradient trajectories.\n\n## 1. Original space and algebraic zero-step isolation\n\nFix a physical length r>=1, h_i>=0 for 0<=i<r, s=sum_(i<r)h_i, and A=1+s.\nAll r+1 formal gradients remain independent. Define\n\n T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,\n e=(1,...,1)^T,\n L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,\n omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,\n D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<r,-s(s+2)).\n\nThe complete hypotheses are\n\n Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for i!=j,\n L^T e=omega,                 D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2=0.       (1)\n\nThere is no support, rank, factorization, or symmetry restriction. Put\n\n x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t,\n Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,\n Qbar_ij=-2Q_ji,\n mu_ij=Lambda_ji/2,\n V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)\n       +sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-sA||g_r||^2.\n\nFact04219554e41d9750 gives the full polynomial identity\n\n              V_h=sum_(i!=j)mu_ij Qbar_ij.              (2)\n\nIt holds for arbitrary formal values and gradients, not only interpolable\ndata. Fact1252b8823bb5a290 fixes Q_ij<=0 on actual normalized smooth convex\nsamples; no actual interpolation assertion is needed for the formal tests\nbelow.\n\nFor i<r let t_i=h_i-1, I_i=sum_(j<i)mu_ji, J_i=sum_(j>i)mu_ji. Matching the\nf_i coefficient in (2) gives out_mu(i)-in_mu(i)=h_i. Matching ||g_i||^2 gives\n\n                  t_i J_i-I_i=(1-t_i^2)/2.              (3)\n\nIndeed the squared differences contribute -h_i-2I_i-2J_i, and precisely\nthe terms Qbar_(j,i) with j>i contribute the additional diagonal 2h_i J_i.\nFor 0<h_i<=1 the left side of (3) is nonpositive and the right side is\npositive. Thus every positive step exceeds1. If h_i=0, (3) makes I_i+J_i=0;\nall incoming mu entries vanish, and zero net flow makes every outgoing\nentry vanish. The entire original Lambda row and column at i are zero.\n\nList the positive-step indices increasingly and retain the original terminal\nr. Restriction to those indices preserves (1): zero steps contribute no\ntrajectory displacement, and every removed multiplier row and column was\nalready zero. Conversely every removed T column is its coordinate vector,\nand the corresponding L row and column are zero, so the full remainder\nextends by zero on those independent coordinates. This is algebraic\nrelabeling only; no physical iteration is removed from a horizon comparison.\n\nIf all steps vanish, Lambda=0 and there is no nonempty positive interval.\nHenceforth r denotes the relabeled positive length, so h_i>1 and s>0.\nSubstitution of the unit quadratic with g_0=1, g_(i+1)=-t_i g_i gives\nV_h=1-A^2 g_r^2 and all Qbar_ij=0. Thus A product_(i<r)t_i=1. This global\nrestriction will not be substituted for the unproved interval restriction.\n\n## 2. The normalization is forced by the full matrix identity\n\nLet S_i=sum_(t<i)h_t and a_i=1+S_i, including S_r=s and a_r=A. Let\n\n o_i=sum_j Lambda_ij,                  c_i=sum_j Lambda_ji.\n\nFor i<r, flow gives c_i=o_i+2h_i>0. The terminal matrix entry in (1) is\n-s(s+2)+o_r=0, since the terminal column of T is its coordinate vector.\nConsequently\n\n                  o_r=s(s+2),          c_r=s^2.         (4)\n\nWrite R0=s(s+2). In particular the terminal normalizers below are positive.\n\nThe full matrix equation, applied to e, gives\n\n                   L a=-2D e-T^T omega.\n\nFor i<r, (T^T omega)_i=2h_i(S_i+h_i-1), while the terminal entry is2s.\nTherefore\n\n (L a)_i=-2h_i a_i (i<r),          (L a)_r=2sA,\n                 sum_j Lambda_ij a_j=c_i a_i.          (5)\n\nThis calculation uses the full matrix equation and exact flow, rather than\nassuming a subword certificate. Since a_i>0, (5) also implies o_i>0 at every\nretained index. Zero row degree was resolved before any row normalization;\nno irreducibility assumption is used.\n\n## 3. A killed chain with all source and sink masses accounted for\n\nAdd a cemetery state dagger. From a nonterminal i<r use transitions\n\n P_ij=Lambda_ij/c_i for 0<=j<=r,\n P_i,dagger=2h_i/c_i.                                  (6)\n\nBoth r and dagger are absorbing. The row sum is1 by flow. Every retained\nnonterminal killing probability is positive. With epsilon the minimum of\nthese finitely many probabilities, the probability of surviving n more\nnonterminal transitions is at most (1-epsilon)^n. Thus absorption occurs\nalmost surely with finite mean, even if the original array is reducible or\nhas missing edges. The transient submatrix P_U, indexed by U={0,...,r-1},\nsatisfies\n\n G=sum_(n>=0)P_U^n=(Id-P_U)^(-1)>=0.                    (7)\n\nThis follows directly from the geometric row-sum bound; no inverse is\nasserted for the singular full Laplacian.\n\nSet a_dagger=0. Equation(5) says that a is harmonic at all nonterminal\nstates for (6). With boundary values a_r=A,a_dagger=0, uniqueness follows\nfrom the same row-sum bound: two solutions differ by P_U^n times their\nbounded difference, which tends to zero. Conditioning on the first\ntransition gives a harmonic hitting-probability vector, so\n\n             Prob_i(hit r before dagger)=a_i/A.        (8)\n\nStart an excursion by making one INITIAL jump from r to i<r with law\n\n                    beta_i=Lambda_ri/R0,               (9)\n\nthen run (6) until its first absorption. This initial jump is part of every\nedge reward and may not be discarded. Let N_ij be its total number of\ntraversals of the original ordered edge i->j, including the initial jump.\nThe expected visits to i<r are c_i/R0. To verify this, the occupation row\nu obeys u=beta+u P_U, whose unique solution is beta G. The candidate\nc_i/R0 obeys that equation because c_i=Lambda_ri+sum_(j<r)Lambda_ji.\nTherefore\n\n                R0 E N_ij=Lambda_ij                    (10)\n\nfor every original edge, including edges into r and the initial edges out\nof r. The chance of killing at i is 2h_i/R0. It follows that\n\n Prob(return)=s/(s+2),       Prob(killed)=2/(s+2),\n Prob(killing vertex=i | killed)=h_i/s.                (11)\n\nThese formulas also follow from (8): sum_i Lambda_ri a_i=c_r A=s^2 A.\nThus every source, return, and killing weight has been paid.\n\nFor later use put b_i=A-a_i=s-S_i on the original states; b_r=0, and set\nb_dagger=A. Both a and b are harmonic for (6), and b_i>0 for i<r.\nThe two conditional laws are explicit. Conditional on return, the initial\nlaw and subsequent transitions are\n\n beta_i^+=Lambda_ri a_i/(s^2 A),\n P_ij^+=Lambda_ij a_j/(c_i a_i),    P_i,dagger^+=0.     (12)\n\nConditional on killing, they are\n\n beta_i^-=Lambda_ri b_i/(2sA),\n P_ij^-=Lambda_ij b_j/(c_i b_i),\n P_i,dagger^-=2h_i A/(c_i b_i).                        (13)\n\nThese formulas are obtained by multiplying a transition probability by\nthe ratio of its successor's and current state's absorption probabilities.\nTheir row sums and initial sums are1 by (5) and its b counterpart. Finite\nmean absorption persists because a conditional survival probability is\nat most the original survival probability divided by the positive event\nprobability. This proves the needed stopping and summability properties\nwithout assuming reversibility or irreducibility.\n\n## 4. Both interval boundaries become a signed edge reward\n\nFix I=[l,k), 0<=l<k<=r. Define\n\n Pfx=S_l, H=S_k-S_l,\n v_l=1, v_(i+1)=-t_i v_i for l<=i<k,\n p=v_k, q=|p|=product_(i in I)t_i,\n psi_i=1 for i<l, psi_i=v_i for l<=i<k, psi_i=p for i>=k.\n\nThe desired conclusion is (1+H)q>=1, equivalently\n\n                    Delta_I=(1+H)^2 p^2-1>=0.          (14)\n\nUse formal gradients psi_i and choose x_l=1. Define formal positions and\nvalues by\n\n x_i=1+Pfx-S_i, f_i=1/2+Pfx-S_i                       (i<l),\n x_i=v_i,      f_i=v_i^2/2                           (l<=i<k),\n x_i=p(1-(S_i-S_k)), f_i=p^2/2-p^2(S_i-S_k)           (i>=k).\n\nThe original GD displacements hold, including both exterior regions.\nThe only potentially nonzero Qbar residuals are\n\n Qbar_ij=2(S_l-S_i)(1-psi_j)        (i<l,j>=l),\n Qbar_ji=2(S_j-S_k)p(psi_i-p)       (j>=k,i<k).         (15)\n\nFor a left/middle pair, put d0=S_l-S_i. The first residual expands to\n1+2d0-v_j^2-2v_j(1+d0-v_j)-(1-v_j)^2=2d0(1-v_j);\nthe reverse residual is zero. For a middle/right pair with e0=S_j-S_k,\nsubstitution gives Qbar_ij=0 and Qbar_ji=2e0 p(v_i-p).\nFor a left/right pair the two orientations give 2d0(1-p) and\n2e0 p(1-p), respectively. Within each of the three regions they vanish.\nThus all crossings are retained and neither orientation is double-counted.\n\nDefine the original boundary sums\n\n C_L=sum_(i<l,j>=l)mu_ij(S_l-S_i)(1-psi_j),\n C_R=sum_(j>=k,i<k)mu_ji(S_j-S_k)p(psi_i-p).            (16)\n\nThe value of the left side of(2) is\n\n                 (Pfx+1)^2-(Pfx+H+1)^2 p^2.\n\nHere the prefix contribution to 2sum h_i f_i+sum h_i(1-h_i)psi_i^2 is\nPfx^2+2Pfx; the interval contribution telescopes to1-p^2; with suffix\nmass Rtail=s-Pfx-H the suffix contribution is p^2(2Rtail-Rtail^2).\nThe terminal terms are -s(s+2)p^2+2sRtail p^2. Adding them proves the\ndisplayed value. Consequently\n\n Delta_I=B_I-2(C_L+C_R),\n B_I=Pfx[Pfx+2-(Pfx+2H+2)p^2].                        (17)\n\nThese are formal polynomial tests, not asserted convex interpolation data.\n\nFor an ORIGINAL Lambda edge u->w assign reward\n\n R_I(u,w)=1_(u>=l,w<l)(S_l-S_w)(1-psi_u)\n          +1_(u<k,w>=k)(S_w-S_k)p(psi_u-p).            (18)\n\nThe indicators select disjoint kinds of edges. Rewards of killing edges\nare zero. In particular an initial edge r->w with w<l has reward\n(S_l-S_w)(1-p), and must be retained. The reversal Lambda=2mu^T gives\n\n        sum_(u,w)Lambda_uw R_I(u,w)=2(C_L+C_R).         (19)\n\nLet Gamma_I be the sum of(18) over the entire killed excursion, including\nits initial terminal jump. The finite mean duration and bounded rewards\njustify summing expectations. Equation(10) yields\n\n                   2(C_L+C_R)=R0 E Gamma_I.            (20)\n\nThe exact missing estimate is therefore\n\n                         R0 E Gamma_I<=B_I.           (21)\n\nEquation(20) alone is not offered as progress closing(21).\n\n## 5. What the maximum principle actually supplies\n\nFor i<r put n_i=sum_j Lambda_ij R_I(i,j), z_i=E_i(sum of rewards before\nabsorption), and J_I=sum_i Lambda_ri R_I(r,i). Let z_r=z_dagger=0. Then\n\n z=G(n_i/c_i)_(i<r),\n R0 E Gamma_I=J_I+sum_i Lambda_ri z_i.                 (22)\n\nBecause b_dagger=A but the zero-boundary vector b_U has both terminal\nboundary coordinates set to0 in the transient equation,\n\n             (Id-P_U)b_U=(2A h_i/c_i)_(i<r).           (23)\n\nDefine the concrete scalar barrier\n\n                 theta_I=max_(i<r) n_i/(2A h_i).\n\nPositivity of G and(23) prove the valid bound\n\n             z<=theta_I b_U,\n R0 E Gamma_I<=J_I+2sA theta_I.                        (24)\n\nNo sign of theta_I is presumed. The last equality uses\nsum_i Lambda_ri b_i=2sA. This is an actual maximum-principle estimate for\nthe actual chain. Section7.2 shows that it does NOT imply(21), even for a\nlegal prefix with B_I=0: its right side is strictly positive there.\n\nThe exact discarded term can be displayed without a qualitative appeal to\npositive or negative edges. Set delta_i=2A h_i theta_I-n_i>=0. Then\n\n R0 E Gamma_I=J_I+2sA theta_I\n       -sum_i Lambda_ri [G(delta_j/c_j)_j]_i.          (25)\n\nHence repairing this barrier requires the quantitative bound\n\n sum_i Lambda_ri [G(delta_j/c_j)_j]_i\n                         >=J_I+2sA theta_I-B_I.       (26)\n\nThe occupation relation Lambda_(r,U)G=(c_i)_i makes the left side equal\nto sum_i delta_i. Thus using source conservation alone cancels the barrier\nand gives back J_I+sum_i n_i<=B_I. It supplies no new signed inequality.\nThis is the precise first failed quantitative estimate, rather than an\nassumption that every crossing edge has a favorable sign.\n\n## 6. Conditioning has a real, generally positive cost\n\nLet E_+ and E_- denote expectations for(12) and(13), respectively. Equation\n(11) gives the exact decomposition\n\n         2(C_L+C_R)=s^2 E_+ Gamma_I+2s E_- Gamma_I.     (27)\n\nFor any original edge u->w, expected traversals multiplied by the indicator\nof eventual return equal the unconditioned expected traversals multiplied\nby a_w/A. This is first-step conditioning after the traversal; absolute\nsummability permits summing it over all visits. The initial edge obeys the\nsame statement. Thus the omitted killing contribution has the exact form\n\n K_I:=2s E_- Gamma_I\n       =(1/A)sum_(u,w)Lambda_uw b_w R_I(u,w),          (28)\n\nand the return contribution has a_w in place of b_w. The weighted bound\nstill required after the Doob transform is\n\n                    s^2 E_+ Gamma_I<=B_I-K_I.          (29)\n\nThere is no theorem here making K_I nonpositive or determined by the\nkilling vertex. Section7.3 proves K_I>B_I>0 in an actual interior-interval\nexample. Section8 proves K_I=8 at a tight interval in a balanced basic\narray, and gives two killed paths with the same killing vertex but\ndifferent rewards. Replacing(29) by a free conditioning argument would\nlose these terms.\n\n## 7. Exact test on the legal array with opposite crossing signs\n\n### 7.1 Full original array and its certification\n\nLet d be the positive root of d^2+5d-4=0 and put\n\n A=5+d, kappa=A^2/16, K=kappa-1,\n h=(1+d,3/2,3/2), s=4+d.\n\nThe polynomial is strictly increasing on [0,infinity), negative at0 and\npositive at1. Hence 0<d<1, dA=4, K>0, and\n\n                       K(4-d)=2(1+d).                 (30)\n\nTo check(30), multiplying it by16 gives\n(A^2-16)(4-d)-32(1+d)=-(1+d)(d^2+5d-4)=0.\nDefine the entire original array in indices0,1,2,3 by\n\n Lambda=\n [0,      0,          0,             Kd              ]\n [3K/2,   0,          kappa,         kappa/2+3K/2     ]\n [3K/2,   7kappa/2,   0,             17kappa/2+3K/2   ]\n [K,      kappa,      14kappa,       0               ]. (31)\n\nEvery entry has the required sign. Row-minus-column sums are\nK(d-4), -3kappa+3K, -3kappa+3K, 6kappa-K(d+2), which by(30) equal\n-2(1+d),-3,-3,8+2d. Thus the full flow holds.\n\nFor the full matrix check, the child(3/2,3/2) has\n\n Lambda_child=[ [0,1,1/2], [7/2,0,17/2], [1,14,0] ],\n L_child T_child=[ [-3/4,-7/4,-1/2],\n                  [7/4,-3/4,-17/2], [1/2,17/2,15] ],\n D_child=diag(3/4,3/4,-15).\n\nIts flow is(-3,-3,6), and its symmetric matrix sum is zero. Joining the\nempty left child to this capacity4 right child with bridge1+d uses\nd(1+4+d)=4. Fact04219554e41d9750, Section11, gives\n\n V_h=kappa V_child-K[(3/2)Q_10+(3/2)Q_20+Q_30\n                           +d Q_03+(3/2)Q_13+(3/2)Q_23].\n\nInsertion of the displayed child coefficients gives exactly(31). Hence\nM=0 on all four original gradient coordinates. This verifies the example\nwithin the joint draft, without adopting the old finding as a premise.\n\nThe chain's c_0=4K, P_03=d/4, P_0,dagger=(1+d)/(2K). Their sum is1 by\n(30). Equation(12) gives P_03^+=dA/4=1: after conditioning on return,\nstate0 deterministically goes to3.\n\n### 7.2 Prefix: a valid maximum-principle bound misses the required sign\n\nTake I=[0,2). Then Pfx=0, H=5/2+d, p=d/2. Only two original edges have\nnonzero rewards and nonzero coefficients:\n\n R_I(0,3)=(3d/4)(1-d/2)>0,\n R_I(1,3)=-9d^2/8<0.                                 (32)\n\nAll initial rewards vanish. Thus n_0=(3K d^2/4)(1-d/2)>0,\nn_1=-(9d^2/8)(kappa/2+3K/2)<0, n_2=0, J_I=0. The barrier is exactly\n\n theta_I=3K d^2(1-d/2)/(8A(1+d))>0,\n J_I+2sA theta_I=3sK d^2(1-d/2)/(4(1+d))>0.           (33)\n\nThe required B_I is zero. Thus the proved comparison(24) cannot establish\nthe desired prefix sign. This is failure of a fully specified expectation\nbound, not just a failed entrywise sign claim.\n\nFor comparison the exact mean, calculated from all edges, is\n\n R0 E Gamma_I=n_0+n_1\n             =-d^2[(15+6d)K+9kappa]/16<0.             (34)\n\nThe positive path3->0->3 has probability Kd/(4R0)>0 and total reward\nR_I(0,3)>0. Thus a pathwise nonpositive-reward repair is also false.\nNo interval counterexample results: (1+H)|p|=2-3d/4>1.\nFor the entire example, the singleton products are4-3d,5/4,5/4; the\ntwo length-two products are2-3d/4 and1; the full product is Ad/4=1.\nAll six interval inequalities therefore hold.\n\n### 7.3 Interior: the killed contribution exceeds the entire budget\n\nTake I=[1,2). Write P=1+d=Pfx, H=3/2, p=-1/2. Both exterior boundaries\nare present. In(18), the nonzero coefficient-carrying rewards are\n\n R_I(2,0)=R_I(3,0)=3P/2,\n R_I(0,3)=R_I(1,3)=-9/8.                              (35)\n\nThe initial edge3->0 therefore matters. Directly,\n\n C_L=15KP/8,\n C_R=-9[2Kd+kappa+3K]/32,\n B_I=3P(P+1)/4,\n 2(C_L+C_R)=B_I-9/16.                                (36)\n\nFor the last equality, use kappa=K+1 and\nK(24+42d)=12P(P+1), which follows from(30) and d^2=4-5d.\nThe hereditary defect is exactly9/16, so this is an admissible interval.\n\nAll negative-reward edges end at3, where b_3=0. The positive edges end\nat0, where b_0=s. Therefore the actual killed contribution in(28) is\n\n                         K_I=15KP s/(4A)>0.           (37)\n\nIn fact K_I>B_I. After division by3P/4 and multiplication by(4-d)A,\nthis inequality is equivalent, using(30), to\n\n 10(1+d)(4+d)-(2+d)(4-d)(5+d)\n                       =d(32+13d+d^2)>0.              (38)\n\nConsequently the return contribution is strictly below -9/16 and is\nessential to compensate a killed contribution larger than the entire\nallowed upper bound. Positivity of a probability law cannot remove this\nsigned compensation.\n\nThere is also a direct positive killed path3->0->dagger. Its probability\nis P/(2R0)>0, and its reward is3P/2. Its reward exceeds B_I/R0 because\nB_I/R0=3P(P+1)/[4(P+3)(P+5)]<3/4 while3P/2>3/2.\nThus a uniform pathwise bound by the required mean threshold is false.\n\n## 8. Exact test on the balanced basic array with two factor channels\n\nLet w=sqrt(2), lambda=1+w, kappa=lambda^2, and h=(w,2,w). Then\ns=2lambda, A=kappa. Consider the full original array\n\n Lambda=\n [0,       2,                 0,                 0]\n [2lambda, 0,                 0,                 kappa-1]\n [0,       (kappa-1)w,        0,                 2kappa+(kappa-1)w]\n [0,       kappa-1,           2kappa lambda,      0].  (39)\n\nIts entries are nonnegative. For completeness a single step w obeys\nV_(w)=-2Q_01-2lambda Q_10, by expanding the two residuals and using\n(w-1)lambda=1. Join two such children with bridge2. Their capacities are\nlambda, and d=1 solves d(2lambda+d)=lambda^2. The accepted join gives\n\n V_h=V_left+kappa V_right\n         -(kappa-1)[w Q_21+Q_31+Q_13+w Q_23].          (40)\n\nThe coefficients after inserting the single-step identities are exactly\n(39). Thus the full polynomial identity, flow(-2w,-4,-2w,4lambda), and\nzero remainder hold on all four formal coordinates. In particular zeros\nsuch as Lambda_30 are retained; no one-factor substitution is made.\n\nTo identify the two-channel example explicitly, for i<j define an auxiliary\nchannel by\nmu_ij=(U_i-U_(i-1))(D_j+t_j D_(j+1)),\nmu_ji=(U_(i-1)+t_i U_i)(D_j-D_(j+1)), with U_-1=D_4=t_3=0.\nAt t=(w-1,1,w-1), the two choices\n\n U^(1)=(1,0,0),       (D_1^(1),D_2^(1),D_3^(1))=(lambda,0,0),\n U^(2)=(0,1,kappa+1), (D_1^(2),D_2^(2),D_3^(2))=(0,lambda,lambda)\n\ngive respectively nonzero entries mu_01=lambda,mu_10=1, and\nmu_12=lambda w,mu_13=lambda,mu_31=lambda,\nmu_23=kappa lambda,mu_32=lambda+kappa+1.\nTheir sum has Lambda=2mu^T equal to(39). The chain analysis uses the full\nsum, not a separate flow condition for either channel.\n\nTake the suffix I=[2,3). Then Pfx=w+2, H=w, p=-(w-1), and Delta_I=0.\nHere B_I=4(w+2)=4lambda w: indeed\n(3w+4)(w-1)^2=w, so the bracket in(17) equals4.\nThe ONLY edge with both nonzero coefficient and nonzero reward is the\nINITIAL edge3->1, whose reward is\n\n                  R_I(3,1)=2(1+w-1)=2w.\n\nSince Lambda_31=kappa-1=2lambda,\n\n       R0 E Gamma_I=4lambda w=B_I.                    (41)\n\nThe initial jump lands at1 with a_1=lambda and b_1=w+2=lambda w. Thus\n\n          s^2 E_+ Gamma_I=4w,\n          K_I=2s E_- Gamma_I=8.                       (42)\n\nTheir sum is4w+8=4lambda w, as required. Ignoring killed excursions would\nlose8 at an EXACTLY tight interval. This is not a small perturbation or a\nlimit, and it occurs in the genuine balanced two-channel basic array.\n\nThe killing-vertex marginal h_i/s cannot determine this reward. The two\npaths3->1->0->dagger and3->2->1->0->dagger both have positive probability:\neach specified original edge is positive in(39), and killing at0 has\npositive probability. Both are killed at0. The first has total reward2w,\nand the second has total reward0. Thus the relevant cost depends on the\nhistory even after the killing vertex is fixed.\nThe three singleton interval products of this word are1,3,1; both\nlength-two products are2w-1>1, and the full product is1. Thus it is also\nnot an interval-heredity counterexample.\n\n## 9. Remaining interface and bounded-round conclusion\n\nThe killed process is well defined for every original array satisfying(1),\nafter the proved isolation of zero coordinates. It retains the original\nterminal source, every crossing entry, both exterior boundaries, all\nalternating weights, and every absorption cost. It requires no irreducible,\nreversible, positive-support, factorized, or inherited-subword assumption.\n\nThe attempted direct estimates have the following precise status:\n\n1. Normalization, harmonic hitting probabilities, finite occupation measure,\n   conditional laws, and full coefficient recovery are derived jointly.\n2. The complete interval crossing expression is the signed excursion reward\n   in(18)--(21), not an interpolable gradient process.\n3. The concrete death-potential maximum principle(24) is valid but too weak:\n   (33) is positive where the required prefix budget is zero. Its exact\n   missing compensation is(26); source conservation by itself is circular.\n4. Conditioning on return leaves the history-dependent term(28). It is\n   larger than the entire interior budget in(37)--(38), and equals8 at the\n   tight basic interval(42). This term cannot be dropped or replaced by the\n   marginal distribution of the killing vertex.\n\nAccordingly the first unproved implication for this mechanism remains\n\n s^2 E_+ Gamma_I\n <=Pfx[Pfx+2-(Pfx+2H+2)p^2]\n      -(1/A)sum_(u,w)Lambda_uw(s-S_w)R_I(u,w),          (43)\n\nfor every interval and every full original M=0 array. Equivalently one\nmust establish(26) from more than the unsigned flow/harmonic accounting.\nNo such signed comparison is proved here, and no full-array violation of\nthe interval theorem is produced. The explicit arrays satisfy heredity.\n\nThis report does not infer a capacity ceiling, a theorem for arbitrary\nM>=0, a neighborhood theorem at seven steps, or a conclusion about the\noriginal unrestricted exact-profile Goal. The earlier scalar extremal\ndraft cannot be used downstream because its full-M0 interval hypothesis\nhas not been established. All new claims remain UNVERIFIED, with no\nfact_submit and no new accepted fact. This completes the one assigned\nmechanism/round; no additional allocation is inferred.\n\n## 10. Retrieval, verification status, and glossary\n\nThe query-memory, search-math-results, direct-proving, and\nconstruct-counterexamples procedures were used. Matlas returned two\ninapplicable results and the finite harmonic-extension Proposition12 of\nYang Chu and Robert Hough, *Solution of the 15 puzzle problem*,\narXiv:1908.07106v1 (2019), https://arxiv.org/abs/1908.07106.\nThe exact source was downloaded and read around the Markov-chain\ndefinitions, Proposition12, and the return-probability proof in Section5.\nThat proposition assumes irreducibility and cites its proof elsewhere;\nthe surrounding application uses a reversible torus walk. Those hypotheses\nare not imported. The finite absorption and harmonic uniqueness arguments\nneeded here were instead proved explicitly by(6)--(8). The reference\nprovides a proof-pattern comparison, not an external mathematical premise.\n\nOperational retrieval errors were exposed rather than treated as success:\nproject-name memory calls failed with DANUS_AGENTS_ROOT unset; the worker-\npinned calls succeeded. A lecture-note PDF download failed SSL certificate\nvalidation and web retrieval timed out. The arXiv PDF downloaded, but\npdftotext was unavailable; its source was then retrieved. Treating the\nsource as tar returned invalid header; inspection identified a single\ngzip-compressed TeX file, which was decompressed successfully and read.\nNo package was installed and no mathematical program was evaluated.\n\nGlossary for local new notation (implicit suffix kc49): L is the original\nflow Laplacian; S_i is the prefix step sum; a_i=1+S_i and b_i=s-S_i are\nthe two absorption potentials; o_i,c_i are original outgoing/incoming\nLambda sums; R0=s(s+2) is terminal departure mass; dagger is the cemetery\nstate; P is the killed transition matrix, P_U its nonterminal restriction,\nId the identity matrix, G its occupation matrix, epsilon the minimum\nkilling probability; beta is the initial terminal-departure law; N_ij is\nthe number of traversals of an original edge; superscripts+/- and E_+/E_-\ndenote conditioning on return/killing. For an interval I=[l,k), Pfx,H are\nits prefix and internal step masses; v_i is its alternating gradient test,\np its signed terminal product, q=|p|, psi its constant/alternating/constant\nextension; Delta_I is its hereditary defect, B_I the crossing budget,\nC_L,C_R the original weighted crossing sums, R_I the signed edge reward,\nGamma_I the total excursion reward, n_i the unnormalized reward drift,\nz_i its expected remaining reward, J_I the initial terminal reward,\ntheta_I the scalar death-potential barrier, delta_i its nonnegative\npointwise remainder, and K_I the killed-path reward contribution.\nIn Section7 d is the specified positive algebraic root, kappa=A^2/16,\nK=kappa-1, and P=1+d only in the interior-example calculations.\nIn Section8 w=sqrt(2),lambda=1+w,kappa=lambda^2; U^(a),D^(a) are the\ntwo explicitly displayed channel factors and do not parameterize the\ngeneral coefficient array. {"id": "138b57940a77871b", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-01T07:55:41.804544+00:00", "author": "max4", "kind": "counterexample", "claim": "Spiral mechanism polynomial-activation shortcut refuted in a joint UNVERIFIED finite-word family: after subtracting the full horizontal budget c_sp S, the necessary additive excess has kappa log E_sp -> pi/2. Scope is the specified spiral zero-height reset geometry only.", "evidence": "# Finite equal-angle obstruction to polynomial activation allowances\nStatus: UNVERIFIED companion to the joint spiral checkpoint candidate cdd7f057606aeb18.\nNo fact_submit or mathematical computation was used.\n\nTargeted claim: for the specified spiral support geometry with a required zero\nminimum at the old reset point, an inverse scale c_sp S+A(kappa), or\nc_sp S+kappa b+A(kappa), can work for every ordinary word with a fixed\npolynomially bounded nonnegative A(kappa) as kappa tends to0.\nThis claim is refuted by the following exact finite family, at draft level.\n\nFix 0<kappa<=1/2 and set epsilon=kappa,\nTheta=arctan(1/kappa), c_sp=sqrt(1+kappa^2)exp(kappa Theta),\nd_sp=1-kappa^2, T_sp=[exp(d_sp Theta/kappa)-1]/d_sp.\nPut e(theta)=(cos theta,-sin theta),\ne_t(theta)=(-sin theta,-cos theta), R_sp(theta)=c_sp exp(-kappa theta),\nv(theta)=R_sp(theta)e(theta), and K=conv({0} union {v(theta):0<=theta<=Theta}).\nThe vector n_sp(theta)=e(theta)+kappa e_t(theta) supports K at v(theta):\nits inner product with v(phi), divided by R_sp(theta), is\nexp(-kappa Delta)(cos Delta+kappa sin Delta), Delta=phi-theta.\nIts derivative is -(1+kappa^2)exp(-kappa Delta)sin Delta, so its maximum\non [-Theta,Theta] is1, at0. It is also strictly larger at v(theta) than at0.\n\nFor any integer M>=1 set Delta=Theta/M, theta_l=Theta-l Delta, and\nA_D=exp(-kappa Delta)(cos Delta+sin Delta/kappa),\nB_D=exp(-kappa Delta)sin Delta/kappa,\nt_0=0, t_l=B_D+A_D t_(l-1),\nr_l=R_sp(theta_l)[(1+t_l)e(theta_l)+kappa t_l e_t(theta_l)],\nalpha_l=1-exp(-kappa Delta)cos Delta+B_D+(1+kappa^2)B_D t_(l-1).\n\nAll t_l>=0 and alpha_l>=0. Resolving radial and tangential components\ngives r_l-r_(l-1)=alpha_l v(theta_l). The supporting-normal calculation\ngives Pi_K(r_l)=v(theta_l). Hence the ACTUAL finite chronological\nordinary word (alpha_M,...,alpha_1) follows these projection gradients\nfrom r_M to r_0=(kappa,-1). Every step is finite; this family is defined\nanalytically, without executing any sweep.\n\nLet S_M=sum alpha_l, P_M=(r_M)_1-kappa, Q_M=-(r_M)_2-1.\nThen P_M=c_sp(1+t_M)-kappa and 1+Q_M=c_sp kappa t_M.\nFor sufficiently large M, A_D>1. Exact summation gives\nt_M=B_D(A_D^M-1)/(A_D-1),\nS_M=M a_D+(1+kappa^2)B_D(t_M-M B_D)/(A_D-1),\nwhere a_D=1-exp(-kappa Delta)cos Delta+B_D.\nThe derivatives at Delta0 imply\nt_M -> T_sp,\nS_M -> (1+kappa^2)T_sp/d_sp-kappa(1+kappa^2)Theta/d_sp.\n\nNow center and scale the finite support envelope to start at (1,0):\nz_circ=(1,0)-sigma(kappa+P_M,-1-Q_M),\nphi(z)=sigma max_(v in V_fin)<v,z-z_circ>,\nwhere V_fin contains0, both endpoint vectors and every gradient just constructed.\nThe terminal local jet is\nx_end=z_circ+sigma(kappa,-1),\ng_end=sigma(kappa,-1),\nf_end=sigma^2(1+kappa^2)/2.\nIt is an actual envelope jet, because (kappa,-1) belongs to K.\n\nIf the old reset point0 is to remain a minimizer with f_star=0,g_star=0,\nthe ORIGINAL directed interpolation row must satisfy\nQ_(end,star)=f_end-<g_end,x_end>+||g_end||^2/2\n =sigma[-kappa+sigma(kappa(kappa+P_M)+1+Q_M)]<=0.\nThis is exactly the endpoint-plane condition phi(0)=0. It forces\n\n    sigma^(-1)>=kappa+P_M+(1+Q_M)/kappa.\n\nThus the excess after paying the FULL available horizontal budget is\nE_M=kappa+P_M+(1+Q_M)/kappa-c_sp S_M, and its exact limit is\n\n    E_sp=c_sp[1+(1-3kappa^2)T_sp/d_sp\n                       +kappa(1+kappa^2)Theta/d_sp]>0,\n    lim_(kappa->0) kappa log E_sp=pi/2.\n\nTherefore a universal inverse scale c_sp S+A(kappa) requires A(kappa)>=E_sp.\nFor the form c_sp S+kappa b+A(kappa), take the legal fixed local\ncheckpoint b=1+kappa^(-2); it requires A(kappa)>=E_sp-kappa b.\nIf A(kappa)<=C kappa^(-p) for any fixed finite C,p, choose kappa small\nenough to violate this strict limiting inequality, then choose a finite\nM large enough. The displayed ORIGINAL row Q_(end,star) is strictly positive.\nThe failed conclusion is the promised preserved minimum/interpolation\ncondition, not smoothness of the support envelope at a different minimum.\n\nThis obstruction keeps the actual finite chronological states and pays\nc_sp S_M-P_M before declaring a deficit. It is stronger than merely\nobserving Q_M is large. It applies only to this support curve and the\nspecified zero-height reset architecture. Positive-height gluings,\nother endpoints/curves, and unrestricted W_n remain open.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"finding_ids": ["9e0d2abd4b36b2b3", "cdd7f057606aeb18"], "artifact": "/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/global_checkpoint_geometry_20261001/activation_obstruction.md", "target_claim": "uniform polynomial additive activation cost for the selected spiral minimum-reset construction", "status": "refuted_in_unverified_joint_draft", "fact_ids": []}, "glossary": {"c_sp": "Horizontal endpoint magnitude sqrt(1+epsilon^2) exp(kappa Theta) of the selected logarithmic-spiral support curve; distinct from benchmark C_n.", "d_sp": "The positive scalar 1-kappa^2 in the spiral turning bound.", "T_sp": "[exp(d_sp Theta/kappa)-1]/d_sp, a uniform bound for the nonnegative spiral normal coefficient.", "H_sp": "c_sp[1+(1+kappa)T_sp]-1, the uniform vertical displacement allowance for a spiral ordinary block.", "A_sp": "epsilon+(H_sp+1)/epsilon, a sufficient local activation allowance.", "a_sp": "2A_sp/c_sp, a sufficient auxiliary constant in the source-format checkpoint bound.", "R_sp": "R_sp(theta)=c_sp exp(-kappa theta), the spiral gradient radius.", "n_sp": "n_sp(theta)=e(theta)+kappa e_t(theta), a verified-in-draft outward supporting normal.", "E_sp": "The exact limit of the equal-angle finite-word activation excess after subtraction of c_sp S, as displayed in equation(23).", "gamma_match": "[c_sp^2-1-kappa^2]/2 when epsilon=kappa; matches the circular source's horizontal coefficient."}}

The normal e_0-x_*=sigma(1/2,-1,0) is the active first endpoint normal. Every minimizer x therefore satisfies <e_0-x_*,x-x_*><=0. Expanding ||e_0-x||^2 proves\ndist(e_0,argmin F)^2=5sigma^2/4.\nThe proposed all-count lower bound sigma^2[(P_0+epsilon+epsilon^-1)^2+Q_0^2] would be25sigma^2/4 and is FALSE by a factor of five. In a genuine one-stage function the tail constrains x_1<=sigma Q_0; here the genuine tail constrains x_2<=0, and the first constraint is absent.\n\nFor actual trajectory evidence, from e_0 the gradients at the two queried points are\ng_0=(sigma/4,-sigma/2,0),\nx_1=e_0-10g_0=(1-5sigma/2,5sigma,0),\ng_1=(0,5sigma^2/4,-5sigma^2/2),\nx_2=x_1-10g_1=(1-5sigma/2,5sigma-25sigma^2/2,25sigma^2).\nThese gradients follow from the proximal point equal to the local center at local input(1,0); sigma(1/2,-1) is an active subgradient. At each query all other local terms vanish. At x_2 both local terms vanish and F(x_2)=(25sigma^2)^2/4. Its TRUE payoff is250sigma^2, whereas the false radius would give50sigma^2.\n\nFor smoothness: if z=prox_phi(x),g=x-z, subgradient monotonicity yields ||g-g'||^2<=<g-g',x-x'>; comparison of the minimizing quadratics gives gradient g and the 1-smooth upper inequality. It is OUTSIDE the H_(m,tau) family and proves NO escape on that family.\n\n2. Equal ordinary sums do not fix actual backward outputs.\nKeep epsilon=kappa=1/2 and the same C. Let theta_*=arctan(3/4), R_*=C exp(-theta_*/2), p_*=4R_*/5,q_*=3R_*/5 and A_*=1+2(1+q_*)/C. The backward endpoint is(1/2,-1). {"id": "f7178031162faf25", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-01T15:14:08.993754+00:00", "author": "max", "kind": "counterexample", "claim": "max68 joint UNVERIFIED formal PSD counterexample: on the specified exact-right-copy asymmetric law, ALL left gradients equal1 and ALL right gradients equal0, with x0=(s+U/2)/q, refute every qD<=4/3 at actual m>=1536 even after arbitrary balanced optimum cycles and arbitrary backward optimum triangles. Left finite completions are unrestricted subject to original nonnegative value flow.", "evidence": "This is a companion to the complete joint draft74648afc6ca81e6c, not a separate accepted result. Use the original convention of fact1252b8823bb5a290: Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2, x_i=x0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, xstar=gstar=fstar=0, and M=q||x0||^2-2fn+sum lambda_ij Q_ij. The actual word is H_m at the SAME enclosing m, beta=1-1/(4m), c=(1+sqrt2)(1+1/(4m)), a=beta/c, H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). Balanced optimum cycles add rho_i[||g_i||^2-<g_i,x_i>]. A triangle star->R_i->L_l->star adds t times Q_(star,R_i)+Q_(R_i,L_l)+Q_(L_l,star); on the displayed left-common/right-zero subspace it adds t[(1+p_l^c)-x0], including its x0 cross term. The following complete coefficient and test derivation is extracted verbatim from the SAME joint draft:\n\n## 5. Complete asymmetric base, with unrestricted left completion\n\nConsider the final actual parent (H_j,d,H_j), j=m-1, k=2^j-1. In THIS\nsection d=1+a c^j is the physical bridge, A=sum H_j, t=A+d,\nS=2A+d, D=1+2S. The left queries L_l=l, 0<=l<=k, include bridge L_k;\nright queries R_i=k+1+i, 0<=i<=k, include actual terminal n=R_k.\nLet p_i^c be the child chronological masses, with p_0^c=0,p_k^c=A.\n\nTake any nonnegative finite reference array K^c and nonnegative mu_i with\n\n    sum_v K^c_iv-sum_v K^c_vi+mu_i=2*1_(i=k).              (21)\n\nIn particular sum mu_i=2. Reference child PSD is NOT assumed. Set\n\n    theta=(3A+d)/(2S), s=1-theta=t/(2S),\n    kappa=0 or s/(2d), E=sum_i mu_i(A-p_i^c), 0<=E<=2A.\n\nThe prescribed original rows of the base are\n\n    K_(R_i,R_v)=K^c_iv, a_(R_i)=theta mu_i,\n    K_(R_i,L_0)=mu_i[s-kappa d(A-p_i^c)/A],\n    K_(R_i,L_k)=mu_i kappa t(A-p_i^c)/A,\n    K_(R_i,L_l)=0 for 0<l<k,\n    sum_l K_(L_l,R_i)=kappa mu_i(A-p_i^c).               (22)\n\nAll b_i are initially zero. All left finite rows and optimum attachments\nare otherwise arbitrary nonnegative values satisfying full original flow.\nNo PSD condition on the left child is presumed. Thus these screens grant\narbitrary position-dependent left corrections already present in the base.\n\nAll prescribed signs hold: s>0,theta>0, and at repaired kappa,\ns-kappa d(A-p_i^c)/A=s(A+p_i^c)/(2A)>=s/2. Put\nB_i=sum_l K_(R_i,L_l)=s mu_i+kappa mu_i(A-p_i^c), and\nF_i=sum_l K_(L_l,R_i)=kappa mu_i(A-p_i^c). Right flow is (21) plus\ntheta mu_i+B_i-F_i, hence is 2*1_(i=k).\n\nFor a completely explicit base completion, send every F_i from L_k, set\nall left finite rows0, and take\n\n    a_(L_0)=2s-kappa dE/A, a_(L_k)=kappa dE/A,\n    a_(L_l)=0 for 0<l<k.                                (23)\n\nThe first attachment is at least s; all are nonnegative. Incoming-minus-\noutgoing cross flow at L_0,L_k is precisely (23); other left flows vanish.\nThus (21)-(23) give a complete value-flow array for j>=1. For the empty\nword use a_0=2 separately; for H_1 use the actual one-step array\nlambda_(0,star)=lambda_(1,star)=lambda_(1,0)=1, and apply (21)-(23)\nat subsequent levels. For h=(b) that one-step base has\n\n    M(1/(1+2b))=||x_0-(1+2b)(g_0+g_1)/2||^2/(1+2b)\n                              +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4.\n\nThe actual b=1+a is below sqrt(2)<3/2. This checks its starting calibration;\nno parent PSD is inferred. Subsequent definitions are coefficient formulas,\nnever executed recurrences. The proof next allows all larger left completions,\nnot just (23).\n\n## 6. New full left-block test: Policies I and III fail even with free weights\n\nOn the ORIGINAL formal space choose\n\n    g_(L_l)=v for EVERY left query, including L_k,\n    g_(R_i)=0 for EVERY right query, including terminal,\n    x_0=x.                                                (24)\n\nThe original positions are x_(L_l)=x-p_l^c v and x_(R_i)=x-tv.\nThis is a test subspace of the whole quadratic, not a quotient or a claim\nthat the formal assignment is interpolation-feasible.\n\nLet B_l be total backward inflow into L_l and F_l total forward outflow.\nLeft finite flow is -a_(L_l)-F_l+B_l. Multiplying it by p_l^c and summing\nshows that all left finite position terms contribute\nsum_l p_l^c[-a_(L_l)-F_l+B_l]v^2. The optimum rows contribute\n-a_L xv+sum_l a_(L_l)(p_l^c+1/2)v^2, where a_L=sum_l a_(L_l)=2s.\nBackward rows contribute (sum_i B_i)v^2/2. Forward rows contribute\nsum_l F_l[-t+p_l^c+1/2]v^2. All p_l^c a_(L_l) and p_l^c F_l terms cancel.\nConsequently the full base (9), including every arbitrary left coefficient,\nrestricts to\n\n    M_base(q)=q x^2-2s xv+Gamma_L v^2,\n    Gamma_L=sum_i B_i+sum_l p_l^c B_l-t sum_l F_l\n           =2s+kappa E.                                 (25)\n\nThe final equality uses sum_i B_i=2s+kappa E,\nsum_l p_l^c B_l=kappa tE, and sum_l F_l=kappa E, directly from (22).\nThis exact cancellation is the restrictive cross-block MOMENT of the base.\nIt is not a general identity for asymmetric certificates.\n\nA balanced cycle on a left label contributes\nrho_(L_l)[(1+p_l^c)v^2-xv] on (24); a cycle on a right label is zero.\nA backward optimum triangle star->R_i->L_l->star contributes precisely\nt_(R_i,L_l)[(1+p_l^c)v^2-xv] by (16). These statements include their\nx_0 mixed terms. Let U be the total weight of these left balanced cycles\nand backward triangles, and let J be their sum weighted by (1+p_l^c).\nThen 0<=J<=(A+1)U, and the complete modified restriction is\n\n    M(q)=q x^2-(2s+U)xv+(Gamma_L+J)v^2.                 (26)\n\nThe right balanced weights may be arbitrary; they vanish on this test.\nThe triangle weights and destinations may be arbitrary as specified;\nno Huber budget restriction is needed for the following obstruction.\n\nSuppose A>=1024 and d/A>2/5. Since E<=2A and kappa<=s/(2d),\n\n    Gamma_L<=s(2+A/d)<(9/2)s<(A+1)s.                    (27)\n\nFor q<=0, (26) cannot be PSD since its mixed coefficient is nonzero.\nFor q>0, set v=1 and x=(s+U/2)/q. Full PSD would require\n\n    q >= (s+U/2)^2/(Gamma_L+J)\n      > (s+U/2)^2/[(A+1)(s+U)] >= s/(A+1).             (28)\n\nThe last inequality follows by subtracting s(s+U) from (s+U/2)^2;\nthe difference is U^2/4>=0. This explicitly pays the mixed term: merely\nraising the positive gradient diagonal would miss its simultaneous change.\n\nAlso Ds>t=A+d, so\n\n    qD>Ds/(A+1)>(A+d)/(A+1)\n                     >(7/5)A/(A+1)>4/3.               (29)\n\nThe final inequality follows from A>20. Thus the exact full-space assignment\njust specified is negative at ANY proposed qD<=4/3, regardless of how the\nbalanced and backward-triangle weights are chosen. This proves the failure\nof both explicit Policies I and III and their larger stated classes.\n\n### The actual enclosing-m words satisfy the elementary conditions\n\nFor j=m-1, direct substitution in the physical sum identity gives\n\n    A=2^j-1+a(c^j-2^j)/(c-2), d=1+a c^j.                (30)\n\nThe identity follows from S_(j+1)=2S_j+1+a c^j and S_0=0 by symbolic\ninduction. For m>=1536, 12/5<c<5/2 and a>3/10. For the upper bound on c,\nuse lambda<17/7 and epsilon<1/256 to get c<4369/1792<5/2.\nLet z=(2/c)^j. Since (5/6)^4<1/2 and j>=24, z<1/64. Then\n\n    A/d<1/(c-2)+z/a<17/7+5/96<5/2.\n\nThis proves d/A>2/5. Every child step exceeds1, so\nA>2^j-1>1024. Therefore (29) holds at EVERY m>=1536, on the actual\nunchanged word, not a newly parameterized child.\n\nThe proof is a short full-matrix screen. It does not spend the round on\nan impossibility theorem for general duals, or assert that a feasible level\nexists above this necessary lower restriction.\n\n\nThese are formal full-matrix tests, not actual interpolation-feasible convex-function lower witnesses. The obstruction does not exclude other finite-row changes or general optimum-outgoing certificates.", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"complete_joint_finding": "74648afc6ca81e6c", "plan_id": "615576a0b963657b", "guidance_id": "66aa167542a58193", "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "status": "unverified", "target_claim": "PSD at a useful whole-word level after the specified additive optimum-row repairs.", "candidate_counterexample": "g_L=1,g_R=0 including the bridge and terminal, x0=(s+U/2)/q; exact full restriction(26).", "assumptions_satisfied": ["Every physical step retained.", "Full original nonnegative value flow.", "Arbitrary left completion allowed.", "Balanced optimum cycles and backward optimum triangles may have arbitrary nonnegative weights."], "failed_conclusion": "Entire original quadratic PSD at qD<=4/3.", "impact": "Closes only Policies I and III and the explicitly larger additive classes."}, "glossary": {"U68": "Total added left balanced-cycle and backward optimum-triangle weight in the original left-common/right-zero test.", "J68": "The same weights times1+p_l^c; satisfies0<=J<=(A+1)U.", "s68": "(A+d)/(2(2A+d)) in the specified final asymmetric join.", "GammaL68": "2s+kappa E, the complete unmodified left-common curvature."}}
{"id": "587f6314446f51bf", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-02T03:12:25.582209+00:00", "author": "max", "kind": "counterexample", "claim": "max71 formal original-matrix counterexamples for exactly the three finite-row laws of b29aef4e7da1bade: a positive common-gradient compression is insufficient for the bridge-destination lift; the backward fan has a growing isolated-gradient Schur defect; the retained-objective forward correction and its signed right-terminal reroute have a negative g0 diagonal. These are dual polynomial tests, not interpolation-feasible primal trajectories or lower bounds on W.", "evidence": "# Exact two-block moments and three finite-row redistribution screens\n\nStatus: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max's single\nallocated Sol/max invocation after terminal-invocation baseline70, with\nceiling71 / attempted ceiling72. No formal call, new fact, all-function upper,\nor resolution of the original exact-profile problem is claimed.\n\nThe new compression permits arbitrary directed finite rows, both optimum\ndirections, and arbitrary changes inside either child. It is derived from the\noriginal quadratic, not substituted for it. Three actual coefficient laws are\nthen specified and screened:\n\n1. Moving the backward destination to the bridge repairs the old coarse\n   obstruction. Its original-Silver calibration is fully PSD at every depth.\n   On the actual perturbed word its full lift retains a negative H4 direction\n   with zero displacement and zero terminal gradient. Raising the objective\n   level cannot repair that direction.\n2. Replacing the left finite block by a moment-matched backward bridge fan\n   removes that inherited finite block. It instead forces a relative loss\n   greater than half a growing physical step, and even an absolute certificate\n   level greater than3/250 at the target horizons.\n3. Retaining the left objective finite block and correcting its individual\n   contact equations with forward-to-bridge rows preserves all signs and\n   value flow. It creates a negative original diagonal already at H2. A\n   budget-valid, signed, nonscalar redistribution of the FINAL right terminal\n   rows through the optimum is included explicitly; it vanishes on the failed\n   left direction and cannot repair it.\n\nThese are scoped failures of THESE arrays. They are not legal primal\nadversaries, lower bounds for W, or general-dual infeasibility results. No\nunproved positive lift or feasible recursive base is left masquerading as a\nresult.\n\n## 1. Physical word, accepted inputs, and original cone\n\nFix one enclosing integer m>=1536 throughout, including every child, and put\n\n    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,\n    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,\n    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), 0<=j<m.\n\nThe actual word is h=H_m of length n=2^m-1. Its terminal gradient is an\ninterpolation variable, not an extra update. The zero-perturbation calibration\nmeans epsilon=0, a=1/lambda, c=lambda, with the same recursive physical word;\nit is explicitly distinguished from the assigned positive epsilon.\n\nAccepted inputs read in this round are:\n\n* fact1252b8823bb5a290: the full original interpolation convention,\n  objective certificate, terminal reduction and full-coordinate scope;\n* fact04219554e41d9750: the unconditional original benchmark and the full\n  primitive/history and objective identities, Sections10--11;\n* fact5fd396eba75651e7: ONLY its scalar benchmark comparison on these words,\n  Section10. Its restricted witness-family optima are not W upper bounds.\n\nThe complete findings74648afc6ca81e6c,83357ebfcaa1356b,\nee71972baf834490,dd4045b39aa0c257 were read as awareness. Their needed\ncoefficient identities are derived again within this ONE joint draft.\nNo unverified finding is an accepted predecessor. Data242f8d453ac26183\nmotivate considering changed finite blocks, but no floating-point equality,\ncoefficient, zero pattern, sign, rank or feasibility premise is taken from them.\n\nUse normalized smoothness1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. The ORIGINAL formal\nvectors x_0,g_0,...,g_n are independent. Write\n\n    x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t,\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.\n\nFor an arbitrary advertised L>0, W_n(h) is the supremum of\n2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2) over every finite-dimensional\nEuclidean space, every convex differentiable f with L-Lipschitz gradient,\nevery minimizer x_star and every x_0!=x_star, with the actual updates\nx_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L)grad f(x_i). Normalizing gives the formal convention\nabove. This is the original W=2R normalization with the specified minimizer.\n\nEvery ordered finite, optimum and terminal row remains in the model. On actual\ntrajectories Q_ij<=0 by fact1252. Put\n\n    K_ij=lambda_ij for finite i!=j, K_ii=0,\n    nu_i=lambda_(i,star), xi_i=lambda_(star,i),\n    S=sum h_i, D=1+2S, P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, h_n=0.\n\nAll FINAL K,nu,xi must be nonnegative. Exact value flow is\n\n    sum_j K_ij-sum_j K_ji+nu_i-xi_i=2*1_(i=n).             (1)\n\nIn particular sum nu=2+sum xi. After (1), the entire original polynomial is\n\n    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-sum_i nu_i<x_0,g_i>\n          +sum_i nu_i[sum_(t<i)h_t<g_i,g_t>+||g_i||^2/2]\n          +sum_i xi_i||g_i||^2/2\n          +sum_(i!=j finite)K_ij[<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2]. (2)\n\nIts original matrix is [[q,-nu^T/2],[-nu/2,C]]. Every entry is\n\n    C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i sum_(j>i)K_ij,\n    2C_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv\n                             +h_i[nu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j, (3)\n    out_i=nu_i+sum_j K_ij.\n\nTo check (2)--(3), each optimum-incoming row contributes its displayed\ninitial-point mixed term and its gradient position term. Optimum-outgoing\nrows have no position term. Half the total incident mass at i, by (1), is\nout_i-1_(i=n), including both optimum directions. A forward position row out\nof i subtracts h_i K_ij from its diagonal. Difference squares and the two\npossible position-row source indices give exactly the off-diagonal in (3).\nNo original coordinate or interpolation pair is removed.\n\nWhen q>0, square completion gives\n\n    M(q)=q||x_0-sum_i nu_i g_i/(2q)||^2\n                       +sum_(i,j)[C_ij-nu_i nu_j/(4q)]<g_i,g_j>. (4)\n\nFull PSD of (2), not just a compression of (4), would imply W_n(h)<=q.\n\nFor orientation, the actual Huber function Hub(x)=x^2/2 on |x|<=1 and\n|x|-1/2 otherwise, started at x_0=D, has g_i=1 and\nf_i=D-P_i-1/2 at every finite query. All finite Q_ij and Q_(i,star) are\nzero; Q_(star,i)=-(2S-P_i). Consequently a necessary paid-row budget at\nq=(1+eta)/D is\n\n    sum_i xi_i(2S-P_i)<=D eta.                            (5)\n\nAt eta=0 this forces every xi_i=0. A tight PSD quadratic must then annihilate\n(D,1,...,1). Direct differentiation of (2), using (1), gives the finite\ncoordinate contact equations\n\n    (2S-P_i)nu_i=2h_i+sum_j K_ij(P_i-P_j).                (6)\n\nFor a derivation, twice the i-th gradient row sum of C is\n2h_i+(1+P_i)nu_i+sum_j K_ij(P_i-P_j): the summed GD displacements use\nthe full flow, and the gradient-difference rows contribute nu_i. The equation\n2Ce=D nu is therefore exactly (6). Equations (1),(6) are algebraic contact\nconditions, not a positivity assertion.\n\n## 2. Exact common-gradient compression with arbitrary finite redistribution\n\nConsider ANY last join (H_j,d,H_j), with k=2^j-1 and physical bridge\nd=1+a*c^j. In THIS section and Sections3,5,6,\n\n    A=sum H_j, t=A+d, S=2A+d, D=1+2S, D_c=1+2A,\n    p_l=sum of child steps before l, 0<=l<=k.\n\nA is a STEP SUM; d is the PHYSICAL bridge, not its excess. Left labels\nL_l=l include the bridge query L_k. Right labels R_i=k+1+i include the\noriginal terminal R_k=n. On the exact original-coordinate subspace\n\n    g_(L_l)=u for EVERY left label,\n    g_(R_i)=v for EVERY right label, x_0=x,\n\npositions are x_(L_l)=x-p_l u and x_(R_i)=x-t u-p_i v. This is a\nquadratic-form test subspace, not a constraint on the original model.\n\nDefine nonnegative cross arrays and their moments\n\n    B_il=K_(R_i,L_l), F_li=K_(L_l,R_i),\n    B=sum_(i,l) B_il, F=sum_(l,i) F_li,\n    pi_B=sum_(i,l)p_l B_il,\n    pi_F=sum_(l,i)p_i F_li,\n    nu_L=sum_l nu_(L_l), nu_R=sum_i nu_(R_i),\n    xi_L=sum_l xi_(L_l), xi_R=sum_i xi_(R_i).\n\nHere B is a cross-row total, distinct from the benchmark B_n. Then the FULL\npolynomial restricts EXACTLY to\n\n    M(q)=q||x||^2-nu_L<x,u>-nu_R<x,v>\n                      +Gamma_L||u||^2+Gamma_R||v||^2+J<u,v>, (7)\n    Gamma_L=B+pi_B-tF+sum_l(1+p_l)xi_(L_l),\n    Gamma_R=D_c+F+pi_F+sum_i(1+p_i)xi_(R_i),\n    J=t nu_R+(t-1)B-pi_B-F-pi_F.                         (8)\n\nThus its three-coordinate matrix is\n\n    [[q,-nu_L/2,-nu_R/2],\n     [-nu_L/2,Gamma_L,J/2],\n     [-nu_R/2,J/2,Gamma_R]].                             (9)\n\nDerivation retaining every within-block row: internal left finite positions\nsum to sum_l p_l[xi_(L_l)-nu_(L_l)+sum_i B_il-sum_i F_li]||u||^2\nby left flow. Right positions sum to\n[2A+sum_i p_i(xi_(R_i)-nu_(R_i)-sum_l B_il+sum_l F_li)]||v||^2\nby right flow.\nAn individual backward row has quadratic coefficients\n\n    ||u||^2/2+(p_i+1/2)||v||^2+(t-p_l-1)<u,v>,\n\nand an individual forward row has\n\n    (p_l-t+1/2)||u||^2+||v||^2/2-(p_i+1)<u,v>.\n\nThe optimum-incoming rows supply nu_l(p_l+1/2)||u||^2 on the left and\nnu_i[(p_i+1/2)||v||^2+t<u,v>] on the right, together with their x mixed\nterms. The outgoing optimum rows supply xi_i||g_i||^2/2. Insert these into\n(2). The p_l nu_l,p_i nu_i and the other internal-source moments cancel.\nAggregate flow nu_L-xi_L=B-F and nu_R-xi_R+B-F=2 reduces the remaining\ndiagonals to (8). This proves (7)--(9) for arbitrary final finite arrays,\nincluding signed changes to their predecessors.\n\nFor xi=0, q=1/D, full PSD would force (D,1,1) into the kernel of (9).\nIt follows that\n\n    pi_B-tF-pi_F=2S nu_L-2t.                             (10)\n\nThe Schur remainder in (9) is then necessarily\nkappa_2*[[1,-1],[-1,1]], where\n\n    kappa_2=Gamma_L-D nu_L^2/4=Gamma_R-D nu_R^2/4>=0.    (11)\n\nIndeed the two row sums of that Schur remainder vanish. Since B-F=nu_L,\nGamma_L-Gamma_R=nu_L+pi_B-tF-pi_F-D_c. Equating this to\nD(nu_L-1) gives (10). This is only a necessary two-block statement. The\nexplicit lift in the next section shows why it is not sufficient.\n\n## 3. Screen I: moving the backward destination changes the moment\n\nUse a child finite objective array K^c and nonnegative attachments mu_i\nsatisfying child value flow and algebraic contact at1/D_c, with sum mu=2.\nNo child PSD is presumed for the failure proof. Start at H_0 with K^c=0,\nmu_0=2. A separate finite source array Lambda^j, defined in Section4,\nhas the canonical primitive flow but is not presumed PSD.\n\nPut\n\n    V=3A+2d, s=t/V, theta=1-s=(2A+d)/V.\n\nSpecify EVERY parent row by\n\n    K_(L_l,L_v)=Lambda^j_lv/D,\n    K_(R_i,R_v)=K^c_iv,\n    K_(R_i,L_k)=s mu_i, with all other cross rows zero,\n    nu_(L_l)=2h_l/D for l<k,\n    nu_(L_k)=2s-2A/D,\n    nu_(R_i)=theta mu_i,\n    xi_i=0.                                             (12)\n\nHere h_l is the actual left child step at l<k. At j=0 the two distinct\nparent queries remain L_0,R_0; the child finite array and child step sum are\nempty, and (12) gives nu_L=nu_R=K_(R_0,L_0)=1.\n\nAll final signs hold. The source Lambda^j is nonnegative by Section4.\nFor A>0, tD>A(3A+2d), since their difference is\nA^2+4Ad+2d^2+A+d>0. Thus nu_(L_k)>0. The other signs follow from\n0<s<1/2, theta>0; at A=0, s=theta=1/2.\n\nLeft source divergence is -2h_l/D at l<k and2A/D at k.\nIncoming backward mass at k is2s. Inserting (12) gives zero left flow.\nRight flow is (2*1_(i=k)-mu_i)+theta mu_i+s mu_i=2*1_(i=k).\nThis proves (1) on every original label, including the terminal.\n\nThis law does not keep the old initial-left-sample anchor or its old right\nattachment scale. Its backward moment is pi_B=2s A, not the frozen moment\nin the prior additive-cycle screens. Its compressed entries are\n\n    nu_L=2s, nu_R=2theta, F=0,\n    Gamma_L=2s(A+1), Gamma_R=D_c,\n    kappa= D_c-D theta^2.                                (13)\n\nThey satisfy (10). The entire coarse matrix has the explicit squares\n\n    ||x-D(su+theta v)||^2/D + kappa||u-v||^2.             (14)\n\nThe positive coarse curvature is a real change, not a renamed Schur cone.\nFor the actual final joins, kappa>0: multiplication by V^2 gives\n\n    V^2 kappa=2A^3-4Ad^2-2d^3+5A^2+8Ad+3d^2.           (15)\n\nWhen 0<d/A<1/2, the cubic part is\n2A^3[1-2(d/A)^2-(d/A)^3]>0, and the quadratic part is positive.\nThe actual final words satisfy this ratio at m>=1536, as proved in Section7.\n\n### The ACTUAL full lift, not only (14)\n\nLet P_j be the complete source gradient polynomial of Section4. Let C^c\nbe the full child gradient block from (3), and define the algebraic child\nremainder\n\n    R^c(g_R)=sum_(i,v)[C^c_iv-D_c mu_i mu_v/4]<g_(R_i),g_(R_v)>,\n    r_mu=sum_i mu_i g_(R_i), p=L_k, x_p=x_0-sum_(l<k)h_l g_(L_l).\n\nR^c is a quadratic DEFINITION; its PSD is not assumed. Direct substitution\nof ALL rows (12) into (2) gives the FULL original identity\n\n    M_I(1/D)=P_j(g_L)/D+R^c(g_R)\n       +||x_p+(A-Ds)g_p-(D theta/2)r_mu||^2/D\n       +(kappa/4)||r_mu-2g_p||^2.                        (16)\n\nFor a detailed coefficient check, the left source identity and its canonical\noptimum completion produce P_j/D and the square\n||x_p-(A+1)g_p||^2/D. Replace the child's right optimum rows by\ntheta Q_(R_i,star)+s Q_(R_i,p). Their difference from Q_(R_i,star)\nis s[-f_p+<g_(R_i),x_p-g_p>+||g_p||^2/2]. The child right starting\nposition is x_p-d g_p. After value cancellation and after its initial-point\nsquare is subtracted, the remaining three-variable quadratic is\n\n    ||x_p||^2/D+2(A/D-s)<x_p,g_p>+(A^2/D+2s)||g_p||^2\n       -theta<x_p,r_mu>+(d-s)<g_p,r_mu>+D_c||r_mu||^2/4. (17)\n\nCompleting the x_p square yields (16): the remaining g_p diagonal equals\nkappa, since sV=t, and its g_p,r_mu coefficient is -kappa. The r_mu\ndiagonal is kappa/4. This verifies every term, including the original\ninitial-point mixed terms, both terminals and every within-block direction.\n\nEquation (16) also proves recursive algebraic contact: P_j annihilates\ncommon gradients by Section4, a contacting child has R^c e=0, and both\ndisplayed squared linear forms vanish at the full Huber vector. Thus the\nparent annihilates that vector without any PSD assumption. Starting from\nthe empty child, (12) defines an actual flow/contact array at every depth.\n\n### Feasible original-Silver calibration\n\nAt epsilon=0, Section4 gives P_j=0 identically. Also\nd=1+(A+1)/lambda. Put u=1/lambda, so u^2+2u=1 and1/4<u<1/2.\nSubstituting d=uA+1+u into (15) gives\n\n    V^2 kappa=2(1-u)A^3+(6-8u)A^2\n                     +(1+u)(8u-2)A+(1+u)^2(1-2u)>0.   (18)\n\nEvery displayed coefficient is positive, including at A=0. Starting from\nthe empty full certificate ||x_0-g_0||^2, (16) inductively gives full PSD\nat every original-Silver depth: Schur completion of a PSD child at positive\n1/D_c makes R^c PSD; all other terms are positive squares. This is a joint\ncalibration proof, not a claimed fact for the perturbed words.\n\nFor every actual enclosing m, the one-step base is also fully PSD:\n\n    M_1(1/(1+2b))=||x_0-(1+2b)(g_0+g_1)/2||^2/(1+2b)\n                                      +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4,\n    b=1+a<sqrt(2)<3/2.                                 (19)\n\nNevertheless (16) has an unpaid full left source polynomial. Section4 gives\nan explicit actual original direction where every term except P_j/D\nvanishes and P_j<0. Thus (12) fails despite (14), without relying on any\nunproved recursive child feasibility.\n\n## 4. Complete source array and a zero-boundary H4 direction\n\nIn this section use capacity notation\n\n    A_j=1+sum H_j, d_j=a*c^j,\n    A_(j+1)=2A_j+d_j, physical level-j bridge=1+d_j.\n\nHere d_j is the bridge EXCESS, different from d in Section3. For terminal z\ndefine the original history polynomial\n\n    V_j=2sum_(i<z)h_i(f_i-f_z)\n              +sum_(i<z)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-A_j(A_j-1)||g_z||^2.\n\nFrom Lambda^0=0, at each join with bridge p and terminal z, embed the\nentire left array with weight1 and right array with weight k_j, and add\n\n    u_j sum_(i right, i<z)h_i Q_(i,p)\n      +v_j sum_(i right, i<z)h_i Q_(i,z)\n      +w_j Q_(z,p)+z_j Q_(p,z),                         (20)\n    u_j=2lambda,\n    v_j=[u_j d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,\n    k_j=1+(u_j+v_j)/2,\n    z_j=2A_j,\n    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-u_j(A_j-1)\n                         =2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda).\n\nAll repetitions in (20) are aggregated onto their actual directed row. This\ndefines Lambda^j, not an executed recurrence. Define P_j by\n\n    V_j+sum Lambda^j_iv Q_iv=P_j(g).                    (21)\n\nThe values cancel: writing A=A_j,d=d_j,s_c=A-1, the two required\nidentities are u_j+v_j=2(k_j-1) and u_j s_c+w_j-z_j=2(A+d).\nThese imply source flow out-in=-2h_i at nonterminals and2(A-1) at\nthe terminal. No optimum row is in this finite source array.\n\nFor a full quadratic audit, write r=sum of actual right steps times their\nnonterminal gradients, T=2A+d, K=A(A-1), K_plus=T(T-1). The four\ngradient polynomials in the order of (20), BEFORE multiplication, are\n\n    d<r,g_p>+||r||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2+s_c||g_p||^2/2;\n    -||r||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2+s_c||g_z||^2/2-<r,g_z>;\n    d<g_z,g_p>+<g_z,r>+(||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2)/2;\n    -(d+1/2)||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2/2-<g_p,r>-<g_p,g_z>.\n\nAdding the history differences gives, with t_j=d_j/A_j, t_star=1/lambda,\nY=r-(A_j-1)g_z and Z=g_p-g_z, the exact full identity\n\n    P_(j+1)=P_j^left+k_j P_j^right+E_j,\n    E_j=(t_star-t_j)[(lambda+1)||Y||^2-2lambda A_j<Y,Z>\n                                    +A_j^2(2+t_star+t_j)||Z||^2]. (22)\n\nFor example the ||r||^2 coefficient is (u_j-v_j)/2, the r,g_p\ncoefficient is u_j d_j-z_j, and the r,g_z coefficient is w_j-v_j.\nThey respectively equal (lambda+1)(t_star-t_j),\n-2lambda A_j(t_star-t_j), and the coefficient obtained by expanding Y,Z\nin (22). The bridge diagonal before substitution is\nK-d-d^2+(u_j s_c+w_j)/2-(d+1/2)z_j, and the terminal diagonal is\n-K_plus+k_j K+v_j s_c/2+(w_j+z_j)/2. Substitution supplies precisely\nthe two diagonals of (22). This checks all coefficients.\n\nThe bracket in (22) is positive definite in Y,Z. Its determinant divided by\nA_j^2 is (lambda+1)(2+t_star+t_j)-lambda^2>=lambda>0; its first\ndiagonal is positive. Every common-gradient vector is annihilated by P_j,\nby (22) and the empty base, because Y=Z=0 for those vectors.\n\nTo establish the actual ratios and every sign without using a previous\nunverified obstruction, put v_h=a/(c-2). Since c(c-2)>1>beta,\n0<v_h<1, and symbolic substitution into the physical sum equation gives\n\n    A_j=v_h c^j+(1-v_h)2^j,\n    t_j=(c-2)/[1+(1/v_h-1)(2/c)^j].                     (23)\n\nThus t_j increases strictly. Its first three levels obey\n\n    A_0=1,d_0=a; A_1=2+a,d_1=beta;\n    A_2=4+a(2+c),d_2=beta c,\n    t_0<t_star, t_1<t_star, t_2>t_star.                  (24)\n\nFor the middle sign,\nlambda d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0.\nFor the last, elementary expansion gives\n\n    c(lambda d_2-A_2)=epsilon[(lambda+4)-(4lambda+2)epsilon\n                                            -(5lambda+2)epsilon^2]>0. (25)\n\nFor 2<lambda<3 and epsilon<=1/12 its bracket exceeds\n6-14/12-17/144>0. Consequently t_j>t_star for every j>=2.\nThe weights u_j,v_j,z_j,k_j are positive. At j=0, w_j=4+2a>0;\nat j=1, A_1<lambda makes w_j>2lambda-2>0;\nat j>=2, w_j>2lambda. Every final source multiplier is nonnegative.\nAt epsilon=0, all t_j=t_star and (22) gives P_j=0 identically,\nin agreement with fact042's original primitive identity.\n\nNow take an ORIGINAL H3 gradient vector w with entries1 at0,1,2,3\nand0 at4,5,6,7. Both children in (22) have zero P_2 value: the left is\ncommon-gradient, the right is zero. Its full value is\n\n    P_3(w)=A_2^2-2A_2d_2-d_2^2=-E,\n    E=(lambda d_2-A_2)(A_2+d_2/lambda)>0.                (26)\n\nThe physical displacement on this support is T_w=A_2+d_2. In H4 set\n\n    v_i=1 at i=0,1,2,3;\n    v_i=-1 at i=8,9,10,11;\n    v_i=0 at EVERY other index0,...,15.                 (27)\n\nThese are actual query indices. The bridge7 and terminal15 are zero.\nThe two signed supports have the identical physical steps, so\nsum_(i<15)h_i v_i=0. At its H4 join, Y=-T_w,Z=0, so (22) gives\n\n    P_4(v)=-(1+k_3)E-(t_3-t_star)(lambda+1)T_w^2<0.      (28)\n\nFor any H_j with j>=4, place (27) in its FIRST actual H4 subword and set\nevery later gradient to zero. At each enclosing source join the root,\nterminal and right block are zero. Equation (22) then gives exactly\nP_j(v)=P_4(v), not a conjectured or numerical amplification.\n\nFinally at the top join of H_m, set x_0=0, give its left H_(m-1) these\ngradients, and set every right gradient, including the actual terminal, to\nzero. For every m>=5 the original left terminal is zero, its displacement\nis zero, and x_p=0. Hence EVERY term of (16) except its first vanishes:\n\n    M_I(q)=P_4(v)/D<0 for EVERY real objective level q.  (29)\n\nChanging q contributes only (q-1/D)||x_0||^2=0. This is the promised\nfull lift failure after a viable original-Silver baseline and a positive\nactual coarse block. It identifies a within-child direction invisible to\nits terminal, total displacement and the common-gradient compression.\n\n## 5. Screen II: explicit signed replacement by a backward bridge fan\n\nThe next law removes the left finite source rather than trying to pay it.\nRecursively use its own child objective array (K^c,mu) with sum mu=2,\nstarting from empty K=0,mu_0=2. At a join use the same A,d,V,s,theta as\nSection3, and specify\n\n    v_l=2h_l/(2S-p_l), l<k,\n    K_(L_k,L_l)=v_l, with ALL other left finite rows zero;\n    K_(R_i,R_v)=K^c_iv,\n    K_(R_i,L_k)=s mu_i, all other cross rows zero;\n    nu_(L_l)=v_l for l<k,\n    nu_(L_k)=2s-sum_(l<k)v_l,\n    nu_(R_i)=theta mu_i, xi=0.                          (30)\n\nRelative to an inherited left child, its signed finite change is\n-K^c plus the displayed fan. No original row is deleted from the model;\nevery FINAL multiplier has just been specified. This replacement explicitly\nacts on the failing inherited H4 directions of Screen I.\n\nEvery denominator is at least V=2S-A>0. Therefore\nsum v_l<=2A/V and nu_(L_k)>=2d/V>0. All other final signs hold.\nAt L_l< L_k, incoming fan weight v_l is canceled by its optimum attachment.\nAt the bridge, its outgoing fan plus its optimum attachment equals incoming\ncross mass2s. The right flow is the child flow as in Screen I. Thus all\noriginal value coefficients vanish.\n\nThis law is also algebraically Huber-contacting. At l<k equation (6) reads\n(2S-p_l)v_l=2h_l. On the right, subtract the child version of (6):\ntheta(3A+d-p_i)mu_i=(2A-p_i)mu_i+s(d+p_i)mu_i, since theta V=2A+d.\nThe initial-point derivative vanishes because total attachment mass is2.\nAt the Huber vector M(D,e)=0. Homogeneity then forces the only remaining\nbridge derivative to vanish. This proves (6) at the bridge also, without\nasserting PSD. For j=0 the law agrees exactly with (19), so the actual\none-step base passes.\n\nIt fails an elementary original-coordinate test before any long-depth proof.\nFor l<k, (3) gives EXACTLY C_(L_l,L_l)=nu_(L_l)=v_l: there is no\nfinite outgoing row at that index. For q>0 choose only this gradient to be1\nand choose x_0=v_l/(2q); every other original gradient is zero. Then\n\n    M_II(q)=v_l-v_l^2/(4q).                             (31)\n\nThus a necessary condition for full PSD is q>=v_l/4. At q=zeta/D it is\n\n    zeta>=D h_l/[2(2S-p_l)]>h_l/2.                     (32)\n\nNonpositive q cannot be PSD because the original mixed entry -v_l/2 is\nnonzero. The same one-gradient test is part of the whole matrix, not a\npostulated child or quotient.\n\nAlready at the original-Silver H3 calibration, the left child has a physical\nstep h_l=2 at p_l=sqrt(2). Its D=1+2S>2S-p_l, so (32) demands\nzeta>1. It therefore fails this required sharp baseline after its valid\none-step base; no long recursive PSD argument is undertaken for it.\n\nThe same exact test also directly excludes the desired actual target levels.\nAt the final assigned join choose the root of its left H_(m-1), namely\nl=2^(m-2)-1. Its actual step is h_l=1+a*c^(m-2), which grows without\nbound with m. Equation (32) excludes every bounded whole-word zeta in this\nlaw. More explicitly (23) gives S_m<c^m; at m>=1536, a>3/10 and c<5/2.\nTherefore\n\n    q>=v_l/4=h_l/[2(2S_m-p_l)]>h_l/(4S_m)\n                  >a/(4c^2)>3/250.                    (33)\n\nThe fixed-step original-coordinate test thus excludes any vanishing\ncertificate level through this fan. It is a restriction on THESE levels,\nnot on W. No feasibility at any higher level is asserted.\n\n## 6. Screen III: retain the objective block, correct moments, reroute right rows\n\nThe third law retains its own entire left OBJECTIVE finite block, rather than\nScreen I's primitive block or Screen II's deletion. Its base arrays have\nxi=0 and start from K=0,mu_0=2 at H_0. At each join set\n\n    K_(L_l,L_v)=K^c_lv + 2s mu_l*1_(v=k,l<k),\n    K_(R_i,R_v)=K^c_iv,\n    K_(R_i,L_k)=s mu_i, all other cross rows zero,\n    nu_(L_l)=(A/V)mu_l for l<k,\n    nu_(L_k)=(2d+A mu_k)/V,\n    nu_(R_i)=theta mu_i, xi=0.                          (34)\n\nThis changes every potentially deficient left source individually. To see\nwhere it comes from, retain the left finite block with scalar weight gamma\nand add u_l to L_l->L_k and v_l to L_k->L_l. Value flow gives\nnu_l=gamma mu_l-u_l+v_l. Its contact equation (6) requires\n\n    V u_l-(2S-p_l)v_l\n                   =2gamma t mu_l-2(1-gamma)h_l.        (35)\n\nThe two EXPLICIT choices tested in this draft are gamma=0 (30) and gamma=1\n(34), with respectively u_l=0,v_l=2h_l/(2S-p_l), and\nu_l=2s mu_l,v_l=0. Equation (35) is a diagnostic linking the laws, not\na proposed unsolved feasibility theorem for other gamma. There is no scan\nover gamma and no additional fourth law.\n\nAll signs in (34) hold, since A/V=1-2s>=0 and2d/V=6s-2>0.\nChild finite flow, the displayed additions and attachments give zero left\nflow at each nonterminal and at the bridge; right flow is unchanged as in\n(12). The total left optimum mass is2s, the right mass2theta, hence total2.\nEquation (35), the child contact equations, and the same remaining-bridge\nhomogeneity argument as for (30) prove algebraic contact at every depth.\nThe first physical step again agrees with the full PSD base (19).\n\nBefore its final paid rerouting, this law also has exactly the coarse squares\n(14), since its cross totals and left-destination moment equal those in\n(13). Thus the positive actual coarse assignment does not detect its\nisolated-gradient diagonal failure either.\n\nThe left forward correction has a position cost that the compression does\nnot see. For every l<k, the ENTIRE original diagonal in (3) is\n\n    C_parent_(L_l,L_l)=C^c_ll-2s h_l mu_l.              (36)\n\nIndeed the new optimum attachment decreases by2s mu_l while the finite\noutgoing total increases by exactly that amount, leaving out_l unchanged.\nThe additional forward row to L_k subtracts2s h_l mu_l from the position\ndiagonal. This calculation includes all original rows.\n\nAt the first nontrivial join H2, let b=1+a be the single child step and\nd=1+beta=2-epsilon the new physical bridge. Original\nindex0 stays in the left child, and (36) subtracts a nonnegative quantity\nat each enclosing join. Hence C^m_00<=C^2_00<0 for every m>=2.\nThese are symbolic coefficient identities, never executed recurrences.\n\n### A fully specified signed, nonscalar right-terminal redistribution\n\nTo exercise the additional freedom that the right finite block need not be\nan exact copy, include this change ONLY at the final join, AFTER constructing\nthe xi=0 base arrays (34). Let\n\n    T_out=sum_(i<k)K^c_(k,i),\n    delta_i=eta K^c_(k,i)/[2(1+T_out)], i<k.\n\nReduce K_(R_k,R_i) by delta_i, increase nu_(R_k) by sum delta_i,\nand set xi_(R_i)=delta_i. No child parameter was reset.\n\nFor the original all-split benchmark, fact042 defines\n\n    C_0=B_0=1,\n    C_n=max_(k+l=n-1)Omega(C_k,C_l),\n    B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(C_k,B_l), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n,\n    Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2,\n    G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2.\n\nAll integer splits are retained. Laws I and III\nfail for EVERY q; Law II forces divergent relative loss and (33). These are alternative-state counterexamples only; initialized terminal absorption is not refuted.", "evidence": "# Explicit alternative-state tests for the three terminal screens\n\nStatus: JOINT UNVERIFIED. These tests refute weaker universal claims only.\nThey do not assert that any tested state is reached from e_1.\nThe frozen static function, backward definitions, physical word and true\nradius are exactly those of joint_analysis.md, shared finding8dfaa8cef518215c.\nNo new function, step, computation or formal submission is introduced.\n\nUse its C=beta+sqrt(1+2beta), kappa=1+beta^2, sigma_i, ell_i, A_i,D_i.\nA component vanishes exactly when x_j<=A_j and\nx_(j+1)>=beta*(x_j-ell_j).\n\n## A. Cross-time pairing need not be nonnegative\n\nChoose 2<=i<=k-2 and a physical suffix step h=b_(m-2) satisfying\n\n    h>2kappa+2ell_(i+2)/(beta*sigma_i)\n                                 +2C*sigma_(i+1)/sigma_i.\n    alpha=h/2.\n\nSet x_i=A_i, x_(i+1)=ell_(i+1)-kappa*sigma_i,\nx_(i+2)=ell_(i+2), and x_j=0 for i+3<=j<=k.\nSet x_(k+1)=ell_(k+1)+D_(k+1).\n\nHere is an explicit choice of every earlier coordinate, removing any\nimplicit \"sufficiently negative\" selection. The known proposed update\nof coordinate i is x_i^+=A_i-alpha*beta*sigma_i. Set\n\n    x_(i-1)=min(A_(i-1),\n              ell_(i-1)+min(x_i,x_i^+)/beta)-sigma_(i-1),\n\nand successively for j=i-2,...,1 set\n\n    x_j=min(A_j,ell_j+x_(j+1)/beta)-sigma_j.\n\nFinally replace x_1 by min(x_1,A_1-beta*D_2).\nLowering x_1 cannot spoil its zero-cone inequality. At both the initial\nand proposed next samples every component j<i has nonpositive input and\nedge residuals. Hence its projection is zero, and (6) leaves all these earlier\ncoordinates unchanged, verifying the proposed update consistently.\n\nInitially r_i=0,z_i=kappa*sigma_i, so\n\n    (P_i,Q_i)=(beta*sigma_i,sigma_i).\n\nThe next component has input <ell_(i+1) and output ell_(i+2), so it is\nzero. At j=i+2 the input is ell_(i+2) with nonnegative output, hence zero;\nall later retired components are also zero. Therefore the retired\ncombined-gradient vector before the step is exactly\n(beta*sigma_i,-sigma_i) on coordinates i,i+1.\n\nAfter the step,\n\n    r_i^+=-alpha*beta*sigma_i,\n    z_i^+=kappa*sigma_i*(1-alpha)<0,\n    r_(i+1)^+=(alpha-kappa)*sigma_i-ell_(i+2)/beta\n                                               >C*sigma_(i+1).\n\nThus component i vanishes, while i+1 is in its horizontal cap since\nits output remains ell_(i+2). All earlier components are zero by the\nexplicit selection; later retired components stay zero. The terminal\nscalar remains isolated: its excess after the step is\nD_(k+1)*(1-h/[2(1+S_(m-1))])>0, because\nS_(m-1)>=b_(m-2)=h. Thus no omitted last gradient enters the retired vector.\n\nThe retired combined-gradient vector after the step is exactly\n(0,C*sigma_(i+1)) on these coordinates. Consequently\n\n    <d_after,d_before>=-C*sigma_i*sigma_(i+1)<0.\n\nThis uses the full static function and its physical available h.\nIt refutes universal nonnegativity of cross-time retired gradient\npairings. It does not refute an integrated inequality on the actual\ninitialized trajectory.\n\n## B. No child resets m.\n\nRead accepted inputs:\n\n* fact1252b8823bb5a290: original interpolation convention, all independent\n  formal vectors, full objective certificate and terminal scope;\n* fact04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11: complete primitive/history convention\n  and original composition identities, used only with their actual hypotheses;\n* fact5fd396eba75651e7, Section10: ONLY the original scalar benchmark comparison.\n  Its restricted witness-family optima are not upper bounds for W.\n\nComplete b29aef4e7da1bade and identical full evidence587f6314446f51bf,\nscoped5f23d331e28e2b91, elaborationd0da7eab62ffb4e1 and guidance\nae616dd4bc25205f were read as awareness. This is W=2R with a specified minimizer.\nOn actual instances every Q_ij<=0 by fact1252.\n\nWrite K_ij for finite multipliers, nu_i for i->star and xi_i for star->i,\nK_ii=0, S=sum h, D=1+2S, Ppos_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, h_n=0. All FINAL multipliers\nmust be nonnegative. Full value flow is\n\n    sum_j K_ij-sum_j K_ji+nu_i-xi_i=2*1_(i=n).             (1)\n\nAfter that cancellation the complete original polynomial is\n\n    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-sum_i nu_i<x_0,g_i>\n       +sum_i nu_i[sum_(t<i)h_t<g_i,g_t>+||g_i||^2/2]\n       +sum_i xi_i||g_i||^2/2\n       +sum_(i!=j)K_ij[<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2].    (2)\n\nIts matrix is [[q,-nu^T/2],[-nu/2,C]], with\n\n    C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i*sum_(j>i)K_ij,\n    2C_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j*sum_(v>j)K_iv\n                              +h_i[nu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j,\n    out_i=nu_i+sum_j K_ij.                               (3)\n\nIndeed half the incident mass, by (1), is out_i-1_(i=n). A forward row\nout of i subtracts h_i*K_ij from its diagonal. Difference squares supply\n-(K_ij+K_ji) to twice the off-diagonal; the GD position sums at sources i\nand j give the other two terms. These observations prove (2)--(3) on ALL\noriginal coordinates. Full PSD, together with (1) and final signs, would\ngive W<=q. No compression is substituted for that test.\n\nFor xi=0, the original Huber data x_0=D,g_i=1,f_i=D-Ppos_i-1/2 have M(1/D)=0.\nFull PSD at 1/D must annihilate this vector. Its contact equations are\n\n    (2S-Ppos_i)*nu_i=2h_i+sum_j K_ij*(Ppos_i-Ppos_j).    (4)\n\nTo check (4), twice the i-th row sum of C is\n2h_i+(1+Ppos_i)nu_i+sum_j K_ij(Ppos_i-Ppos_j); equate it to D*nu_i.\nAll three laws below have xi=0 and exact contact. Define its history polynomial\n\n    V_j=2sum_(i<z)h_i(f_i-f_z)\n             +sum_(i<z)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2\n             -A_j(A_j-1)||g_z||^2.\n\nFrom Lambda^0=0, at each join with bridge p and terminal z embed the left\narray with weight1 and the right array with weight k_j, and add exactly\n\n    u_j*sum_(i right,i<z)h_i Q_(i,p)\n     +v_j*sum_(i right,i<z)h_i Q_(i,z)\n     +w_j Q_(z,p)+z_j Q_(p,z),                          (5)\n    u_j=2lambda,\n    v_j=[u_j*d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,\n    k_j=1+(u_j+v_j)/2,\n    z_j=2A_j,\n    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-u_j(A_j-1)\n                          =2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda).\n\nRepeated contributions are aggregated onto their actual rows.
